# ANCHORS — where each system lives, by subsystem

The encyclopedia that used to be CLAUDE.md's anchor list, grouped by subsystem instead of by version,
with the file each anchor is in and today's names (v149 C renamed the career app; `docs/NAMES.md` maps
old → new, and old names appear here once, in parentheses, where they still show up in comments).

**Find a system:** `grep -rn "v146 E THE MENUS" src/` — banners are `/* ===== vNN NAME ===== */`. An anchor
is the text between the backticks at the start of a line below; `node scripts/anchorcheck.mjs` proves each
one still occurs in the file(s) named after the first `—` (so a renamed banner fails the smoke suite, not a
reader). History and rationale: `docs/CHANGELOG.md`. Deep dives: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`.

Entry format (the check parses it): `` - `ANCHOR` / `ANCHOR` — `file` + `file` · what it is ``. Put a new
entry at the TOP of its section. Hooks are `window.*` names (the public API — never renamed); checks are
`scripts/<name>.mjs` (their suites: `node scripts/run-checks.mjs --list`).

Files: `03` splash/film · `04` engine (tunables, choreographer, FieldSim) · `05` broadcast renderer ·
`07` career app (state, screens, season, prestige, the game engine `simGameV2`) · `11` pregame wizard ·
`17` pregame wheel · `18` growth wheel · `22` hub sections · `25` shell · `26` platform · `27` monetize —
all `src/NN-*.js`; `public/rib-menu*.js|css` menu + coach; `public/rib-vault*` vault. See `docs/LAYOUT.md`.

---

## 1. Engine — FieldSim, contact, the throw (`src/04-engine.js`)

- `v194 A OUT OF BOUNDS` / `v194 A THE MESH` — `src/04-engine.js` (+ the picture in `src/05-field-renderer.js`, the game side in `src/07-career-app.js`) · Beside `rollBlockV81` (the helpers), in the carry's lane read (the pitch's sweep `sweepEdgePxV194A`/`sweepTurnLxV194A`/`sweepFwdKV194A`; the opt-in bounce `bounceV194A` 0), the v41 sideline economy (THE STEP-OUT: `oobEdgePxV194A` 100 / `oobGapV194A` 75 / `oobBailPV194A` .85, re-read every `oobReadMsV194A`, the clock from the game's `opts.clockV194A` — trailing late widens it `oobEdgeOutPxV194A`/`oobBailOutPV194A`, leading late stays in `oobBailInPV194A`; he turns for the paint at `oobOutFwdKV194A`), the v103 grip tick (THE PUSH-OUT: rolled once a grip inside `pushOutZonePxV194A` 40, `pushOutPV194A` .6 ± leverage/strength/intent; never a man in his beaten window), the sideline crossing (a step-out names NO tackler), the post-whistle coast (`oobRunOutPxV194A` over the paint) and `fitLogYardsV139` (a cut out-of-bounds play still crosses, or becomes the in-field stop it shows: `oobCutMaxPxV194A`). A pitch is read by the perimeter at `pitchReadMsV194A`. THE MESH: `meshTickV194A` / `meshReadyV194A` in the handoff phase — the QB goes to the back, the back to his hip; the ball changes hands only within `meshContactPxV194A` 8 and `meshLevelPxV194A` level (failsafe `meshMaxLateMsV194A`), the event says `mesh:true, d` (a pitch, the `toss|jet|pnp|rev` calls, says `pitch:true`). 05: `meshV194A(e)` / `pitchV194A(e)` — a meshed handoff is the hand cycle, flat (`meshHandMsV194A`), no ribbon; a step-out pops "OUT OF BOUNDS" and is not shoved. 07: `clockCtxV194A`, `oobTxtV194A`; a resolved row is out of bounds only if the sim put it out. Kill switch `TU("v194A", 0)` (and `meshV194A`, `pushOutV194A`, `sweepV194A`, `pitchReadV194A`). `root.__V194A` (`mesh`, `oob`). `v194Acheck`.
- `v194 D THE STIFF ARM (the sim's grade)` — `src/04-engine.js` · In `contact()`'s move resolution, beside the stiff-arm roll: `stiffGradeV194D(edge, extra)` grades every stiff arm off the numbers that rolled it — the carrier's strength edge over the tackler (`armEdge`, from `stiffPowLoV194D` over `stiffPowSpanV194D`) plus the momentum mismatch (`stiffPowMomKV194D`) → `pow` 0-1, `drop` (`stiffDropV194D` .5), `sick` (`stiffSickV194D` .8) — on the `stiffarm` event; a TRUCK by a back who chose the arm (v166 F `cmMove` "stiff") is emitted as the same `brokenTackle` with `stiffArm: true` and a grade (`stiffTruckPowV194D`, `v194Dtruck`). **No roll is added and nothing physical moves** (seeded games are identical on and off); the stiff-armed man is `beaten`, never the tackler. Kill switch `TU("v194D", 0)`. `v194Dcheck`.

- `v193 AJ PHYSICS THE BODY HAS MASS` — `src/04-engine.js` · Above `evolveSpeed`, in it, in `mv` and in `endTackle`. `massKV193(a, pow)` reads the body's mass off FieldSim's own WT table (`MASS_V193`, `massRefV193`, clamped `massKMinV193`..`massKMaxV193`): a heavy man brakes softer (`brakeRate` / k^`massBrakePowV193`) and loses more speed to a cut (k^`massTurnPowV193`, the loss already scales with the angle); `massCarryPowV193` (heading inertia in `mv`, default 0) and `decelV193` (the ground covered obeys the brake — a man runs THROUGH a spot he cannot stop on, `brakeStepV193`; default 0) exist but are off because they moved the balance (+1 yd a pass, +1.9 yd a carry). An un-gripped carrier falls forward by his momentum against the opposing part of the tackler's (`fallFwdKV193`, cap `fallFwdMaxPxV193`). The pass's height is a parabola in time (`ajBallApexFV193`) under one gravity (`apexPxV109` drops the style factors, cap `ajArcCapV193`) — presentation only. No random draw. Kill switch `TU("v193AJphys", 0)`. `root.__V193AJP` (`brake` by mass, `turns`, `falls`, `fwdPx`, `maxDrop`). `v193AJcheck`.

- `v177 A THE SCRUM SHOVES` / `v177 B HE SPINS OUT AND RESETS` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · Beside `gangDownV153A` and in the v103 grip tick (04), beside `shoveV164H` and in the event switch (05). Through a grip `scrumCrowdV177A` keeps the field moving (everybody coasts on his line, `gripCoastBrakeV177A`; never a chase — it cost YAC; a pocket sack keeps the old grip) — the old tick `continue`d past everyone. When the grip would land with 2+ defenders in it (`scrumMinDefV177A`) `scrumStartV177A` turns it into a scrum for `scrumMsV177A`+rand: near men run in and join (`scrumJoinV177A`, `scrumPullPxV177A`, at most `scrumMaxOffV177A`/`scrumMaxDefV177A`), each side's push is a diminishing strength sum (`scrumPowV177A`, `scrumManKV177A`), the net drift is `scrumBiasV177A` + `scrumPowYdV177A` per man-of-push (clamped `scrumMaxYdV177A`), and the pile surges back and forth around it (`scrumSwayYdV177A`, `scrumPerMsV177A`; `scrumSurgeV177A` per reversal) before `landGripV177A` books the tackle — the scrum moves the v153 A spot by exactly its drift; no strip/collar/break inside it; pushers are never credited. v177 B: once per scrum at `brkAt` (`brkBaseV177B` + elusiveness vs wrap × `brkEdgeKV177B`, `brkCapV177B`, cut by `evadeRepeatK`) he breaks free (`breakFreeV177B`): the men on him are beaten `brkBeatenMsV177B`, the carry code takes him back (`brkBackMsV177B`/`brkBackPxV177B`) then out (`brkBounceMsV177B`, the emptier side). 05: `scrumPoseV177A` (lean, run cycle in place, rock). Kill switches `TU("v177Ascrum", 0)` / `TU("v177Bbreak", 0)`. Hooks `window.__V177A` / `window.__V177B` / `window.__V177A_R`. Check `scrumcheck`.

- `v166 F THE MOVE IS A CHOICE` — `src/04-engine.js` · In `contact()`, right before the whiff roll. The carrier picks one move for this tackler (`cmPlanV166F`'s fit: cut where there is room or the tackler is on a bad line, hurdle over a low aim, stiff-arm when stronger or the line is bad, truck on momentum or near the sticks); past `cmIqV166F` (his `visE`) he takes the fitting move, under it his best-rated one. The chosen move's roll × 1 + (`cmBoostV166F` − 1) × its fit over `cmFitSpanV166F` (a forced misfit falls below 1), the others × `cmCutV166F` (`cmK` on whiff, hurdle, stiff-arm, truck). No draws. Emits `carrierMove`. Kill switch `v166Fmove`. `root.__V166F` (`plans`, `fit`, `moves`); `cmovecheck`.
- `v166 E POCKET PRESENCE` — `src/04-engine.js` · The pocket block before `free.forEach` / the QB's `mv` (after v82's spy attack). - `v165 C OPEN-FIELD EYES` — `src/04-engine.js` · In the carrier's lane pick (the `else` of the run-lane read, where the sine weave was). Past the line a carrier projects seven lanes `ofAheadPxV165C` ahead every `ofReadMsV165C` and keeps the one with the most ROOM (the least time any live, unblocked, unbeaten defender needs to reach the spot, less his own), with prices for veering (`ofStraightKV165C`) and the sideline (`ofEdgeKV165C`); he switches only for a lane `ofSwitchSV165C` seconds roomier and his aim eases toward it (`ofEaseV165C` a tick) — a snapping lane yanked the follow cam past v109 E's 70 px step. How much he trusts it is his vision IQ: `visE` under `ofVisPivotV165C` runs the old weave, `ofVisSpanV165C` above it the read outright. No random draws. Kill switch `v165Cof`. `root.__V165B.of` counts the you-player's reads; `iqcheck`.
- `v165 B FOOTBALL IQ` — `src/04-engine.js` · Above `makeAgents`. Every agent carries `awareE` / `visE` / `discE`: the rating, plus the you-player's headroom past 99 (v165 A, `iqHeadKV165B`), plus the level's football sense (`IQ_LVL_V165B` / `iqLvlPtsV165B`, Pee Wee −8 → Interstellar +4). The decisions read them through `awE` / `visE` / `discE`: the QB scan (`_readN` up to `iqMaxReadsV165B`, `_noise` floor `iqNoiseMinV165B`, `_moveOnP` steeper past 70 by `iqMoveOnKV165B` to `iqMoveOnCapV165B`, the move-on margin shrinking `iqMoveMarginKV165B`, `_badReadP` floor `iqBadReadMinV165B`), the lead (`leadSkillV101`), composure, the smart sack, ball-find, the v81 read clock and the fake (`iqBiteMinV165B`), the v29 pursuit angle, the corner's leverage error, the LB drop, the back's lane `sees` (`iqSeesCapV165B`) and cutback. `iqCalmV165B` shrinks a sharp man's per-snap jitter (same draws). FieldSim asks the accessor for his mind keys with a third argument (`att(p, n, 1)`) and banks what lands past 99. Kill switch `v165Biq` (it also stops the headroom request). `root.__V165B` (`lvlPts`, `lvl`, `awE`/`visE`/`discE`, the counters `qbReads`/`qbBest`/`laneReads`/`laneOpen`/`of`); `iqcheck`.
- `v164 I THE START IS EARNED` — `src/04-engine.js` · Above `evolveSpeed`. A STANDING start (`a._standingV164I`: the first call, a restart after `_launchReady`, or a hard turn at a standstill) climbs a real ramp for `accelLaunchMsV164I` or until `accelEarnedFracV164I` of top speed: `accelBaseV164I` + (accel−50)·`accelRatingKV164I`, × burst (`burstAccelKV164I`), × a taper that eases the last stretch (`accelTaperKV164I`, `accelTaperFloorV164I`), no rolling multiplier; after that the v38 model runs unchanged (cuts, catches, staggers at speed). Braking and turns untouched. The back takes the handoff moving (`meshVelV164I`, `meshRollV164I`), and under center (v164 P) the back WAITS at the mesh (`ucRbPaceV164P` 0 — his running start outran the defense, +8 points a game in `equaltalentcheck`). Measured 90% of top speed at 1.1–1.5 real s (was 0.4–0.6), 0-10 yd split 1.5–2.0 s. `accelerationTest` adds `yd05/yd10/yd15`, `ydTo90`, `split10Ms`. PAST THE WALL: in `makeAgents` `g(n, tail)` gives HIS speed / acceleration / burst / agility a diminishing tail past 99 (`hiTailMaxV164I` 30, `hiTailHalfV164I` 120; the ramp's clamps rise to `hiCapV164I` 130) — sheet 250 → 102.6, 400 → 112.7, 600 → 117.9 (all used to clamp to 99); the same one jitter draw per call. Kill switches `accelV164`, `hiTailV164I`. `accelcheck` (pure Node); balance: `movementcheck` inside ±12%, `equaltalentcheck` 59.3 total.

- `v164 P THE OFFENSE HAS FORMATIONS` / `v164 P THE OFFENSE HAS FORMATIONS (the call)` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/07-career-app.js` · Before `makeAgents` (04) and after `window.__pickPlayV101` (07). `FORM_V164P` names five looks (shotgun, singleback, iform — the tight end is the fullback, pistol, strong) and where slots 8 / 9 / 2 / 10 stand in each; `applyFormationV164P(off, name)` moves the men AFTER the slots are filled (v117) and every log opens with a `formation` event; `sim()` takes `opts.formation` through `makeAgents(…, formation)`. Kill switch `v164Pform` (shotgun, no draw). `root.__V164P` (04: `counts`, `last`), `window.__V164P` / `__formationV164P` (07); `formcheck` (pure Node).

- `v153 A THE PILE GOES DOWN TOGETHER` / `v153 A GIVE GROUND` / `v153 A THE BALL IS SPOTTED WHERE HE GOT TO` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · Beside `wrapInV109` and in the v103 grip tick. `gangDownV153A(c, tkId, ids, gang, hands)` lists the heap on a gang stop (supporters/joiners within `gangDownPxV153A` x `gangDownSupKV153A`, other closers within `gangDownPxV153A` at `gangDownVelV153A`+, nearest first, max `gangDownMaxV153A`; never the tackler; no roll) as the tackle's `downV153A`; contactV146 lays them on the pile ring (`gangRingPxV153A`) and 05's tackle case folds them (`gangFoldLagMsV153A`). GIVE GROUND: rolled ONCE per grip the first tick a group forms (`escapeBaseV153A`, `escapeEdgeKV153A`, `escapeManKV153A`, `escapeCapV153A`, the agi/str/bc weights, `evadeRepeatK`) — the grip opens, the grabbers are beaten (`escapeBeatenMsV153A`), `c._escV153A` walks him back (`escapeMsV153A`, `escapeBackPxV153A`, `escapeLatPxV153A`, `escapePaceV153A`), `escapeV153A` + a `cut` spin (`escapeV153A: true`). FORWARD PROGRESS: `G.fpLxV153A` is the furthest point his progress reached inside the grip; a landing more than `fwdProgMinPxV153A` behind it books from it (`c._fpSpotV153A` in `endTackle`) and the tackle carries `fpX`/`fpYd` (mapped through the log bridge); never a QB behind his line (`fwdProgQbLosPxV153A`). **The escape spends a Math.random() only when a group forms — compare seeds against the OFF spread.** Kill switches `v153A` / `gangDownV153A` / `escapeV153A` / `fwdProgV153A`. `window.__V153A` (sim), `__V153A_R` (drawn); `v153Acheck`.

- `v151 D THE MAN HAS TO GET THERE ON HIS OWN LEGS` — `src/04-engine.js` · End of the choreographer IIFE, before `contactV146`. `approachV151D` replaces v146 A's smoothstep walk-on (which added up to 400-590 px/s on top of a man's sprint — the "flying in"): the named tackler's approach is capped at `tacklerPaceCapV151D` x his own `sp` (now carried on every script actor), and when the play is too short for that the WHISTLE WAITS (`waitV151D`: the carrier's last `waitWinMsV151D` eased out, the hit and everything after it moved later, bounded by `waitMaxMsV151D`, never before he has the ball). Same spot, yards and NAME. The choreographer's closer multiplier (`choreoCloseMultV151D`) and per-frame step (`choreoDefPaceV151D`, ball-holders exempt) are capped too. Kill switch `approachV151D` / `paceV151D`. `window.__V151D`; `v151Dcheck`.
- `v151 D ONE PAIR OF LEGS A TICK` — `src/04-engine.js` · In FieldSim's `mv`: a man's ground per tick is budgeted (`paceTickCapV151D` x `spd`) across every `mv` call that tick — 3% of moves were a second stride in the same tick. `TU("paceV151D", 0)`.
- `v151 D THE HIT IS WON AT THE ANGLE` — `src/04-engine.js` · In `contact()`, beside v143's `angQ`: the approach grade is amplified around its mean (`angleGainV151D`, `angleMeanV151D`) and now also drives truck and stiff-arm odds (`angleTruckKV151D`, `angleStiffKV151D`); a bad-line whiff is an overrun (`overrunKV151D`). Near-zero-sum: stop rate per commit holds, bad line → good line spread 22 → 37 points. A man standing still is untouched (v110). `TU("angleV151D", 0)`.
- `v151 D THE PUSH` — `src/04-engine.js` · In the v103 grip tick: a tackler who clearly won the collision (`pushBackEdgeV151D`) drives the carrier BACK for `pushBackMsV151D` before they go down, both moving together; a dominant carrier's drag is announced the other way. Emits `pushV151D {who,on,dir,edge,ms}`; yards are the ground covered (sim truth). `TU("pushV151D", 0)`.
- `v151 D HE FINDS HIS FEET (the beaten man)` — `src/04-engine.js` · The chasers loop: an overrun brakes (`overrunEndPaceV151D`) and the rest of the beaten beat is a plant-and-turn walk (`plantTurnPaceV151D`) instead of a freeze then a one-tick reversal. `TU("recoverV151D", 0)`.

- `v146 A EVERY MAN WHO GOES DOWN WAS TAKEN DOWN` / `v146 A THE SACK HE TAKES IS TAKEN BY SOMEBODY` — `src/04-engine.js` · No phantom downs. `contactV146(script)` runs on EVERY script (`window.buildPlayScript` wraps it): a named tackler more than `contactPxV146` off the carrier at the last `tackle` gets a smoothstep offset so he is `contactSetPxV146` off at the event, then is glued to the carrier (`fallMsV146`, `pileOnPxV146`) — except a hit stick or launch (`stickThroughMsV146`); a script that ends on the tackle gets a `fallWindowMsV146` coast. It never renames, re-times or re-spots, so any new dead-ball tackle is covered for free — **but a new yardage reshape must still go through `retagSimLog`**. The taken sack braces and waits for contact (`sackContactPxV146`, `sackCloseMaxMsV146`, `sackCloseCapMsV146`, `sackBrace` event) and spends extra random draws — compare seeds against the OFF spread. Kill switches `contactV146` / `sackCloseV146` (and the renderer's `togetherV146`, §2). `window.__V146`; `v146Acheck`.
- `v143 THE TACKLE IS A MOVE, NOT A COLLISION` — `src/04-engine.js` · Beside `hitGeoV109`. Three decisions taken before any roll and written onto `hit`: `angleQV143` grades the LINE he is on (his motion vs the carrier's lead point) and **must be frozen on the approach** — at contact the reading is noise (mean dot .08 vs .63); scaled by his own speed so a stationary man in the gap is not punished. `windupV143` is the gather (time needed vs time had since `windupWatchPxV143`, 64px). `aimPickV143` chooses LOW / MID / HIGH, `AIM_FX_V143` is what each costs (near zero-sum, `wrapQ` the counterweight); `style` still says `"even"` for mid. **The aim costs a `Math.random()`, so ON and OFF are different sample paths even under a fixed seed — compare several seeds against the OFF spread.** `cid` restarts every snap (key logs per play); LOW is chosen against better carriers and the worst-angle bucket is full of chase-downs — both invert a naive reading. Kill switch `TU("v143", 0)`. `window.__V143`; `v143check`.
- `v129 THE BALL IN STRIDE` — `src/04-engine.js` · Beside `leadSkillV101`. `strideOddsV129(qb, target, sep)` is how often the passer commits to the spot: `STRIDE_LVL_V129[level]` (0 at Pee Wee, a staple in the UFF) moved by awareness, arm, receiver speed and separation; decided BEFORE `leadPointV101`, never under pressure / off-platform / panicking. When it lands `_lk` → ~1.0, the cone × `strideConeK`, the cap opens to `strideOverYd`, the receiver tracks at `strideTrackMul`. Catch/pick/swat rolls untouched. `TU("strideV129",0)` off; the `throw` event carries `stride`/`strideOdds`. `window.__V129`; `stridecheck`.
- `v117 ONE MAN, ONE SLOT` / `v117 AND HE ROTATES THROUGH THEM` — `src/04-engine.js` · The eleven markers are drawn from a per-position pool and the draw REMOVES what it hands out (`placed`, `drop()`); the carrier, target, QB and the you-player reserve slots first, and the you-player's `youSlot` is rolled among his position's slots each snap. Fixed the you-player fielded as two or three LBs (56% of defensive snaps) and credited with team-mates' stops. An interception is `pe(X.cover)` and nothing else; a face mask / horse collar on YOU marks the row `involved`. `v117check`.
- `v112 THE HIT HAS WEIGHT` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · `launchV112` answers one number off figures `contact()` already computed (no roll, no state, no spot moved); the renderer derives hang and peak from that one `flyVz`, and the drawn body LAGS the script's position rather than inventing ground. `window.__V112_F` / `__V112_F_SIM`; `v112Fcheck`.
- `v110 THE MAN WHO IS THERE` — `src/04-engine.js` · Position decides who makes the play: a read lands once the ball is inside `TU("seeBallPx", 20)`; support holds only if genuinely farther off than the committer; the commit goes to whoever is closest after the step (`commitTakePx`); a defender the carrier runs into resolves contact with no commit (`contactAnyPx`); at the catch point the nearest defender plays the ball and takes the credit (`ballManTakePx` / `ballManReachPx`, `out.coverPlayer`). Support CLOSES instead of parking. `window.__V110`; `v110check`.
- `v109 THE BALL HAS A SPEED` / `v109 THE HIT HAS A POINT` / `v109 THE FEET PLANT` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · The realism suite (the clock is in §2b). The throw is a release + velocity (`flightMsV101` rewritten, `ballVelV109`, `apexPxV109`, arc peaking at `TU("arcApexFrac")`); the `throw` event carries `dur/vel/velMph/apex/wobble/platform/errDir`; a hurried miss is biased behind-and-short (magnitude untouched, so `catchP`/`intP`/`swatP` hold); a throwaway can land out of bounds. Contact carries geometry (`hitGeoV109`: `cid`, `ix/iy`, `nx/ny`, `impact`, `side`), the gang converges (`wrapInV109`), a strip runs a real loose-ball scramble to the SAME pre-rolled recovery (`recoverV109`; `looseV109` off restores the old ending), a hard hit can bobble. Feet gather and plant before a cut (`plantV109`), men lean (`leanV109` / `faceAngV109`), a trucked man slides and gets up on a clock. **Score-neutral by construction** — `scoreneutralcheck` is the gate; `v109Acheck` … `v109Echeck`.
- `v103 THE GRAB` / `v103 THE GRIP TICK` / `v103 THE LINE BLOCKS FOR HIM` / `v103 THE TRENCH BREAKS UP` — `src/04-engine.js` · A landed wrap opens `c._grip` and returns `"grip"`, not `"tackle"`; the grip tick at the top of the carry block travels the pair, piles men on, strips the ball (`out.fumble`, booked as a NAMED turnover), strains for the sticks, breaks, and finally emits the tackle with `dragged/dragYd/strain`. Renderer side in §2. `window.__V103`; `v103check`.
- `v101 THE LEAD` / `v101 THE POCKET IS A NUMBER, AND SO IS PANIC` — `src/04-engine.js` · The throw: `leadPointV101` / `walkRouteV101` / `leadSkillV101` / `coneYdV101` / `protV101` / `windowV101` (`window.__V101.last` is the last one). The playbook that feeds it is in §1b.
- `v96 THE READ RADIUS` — `src/04-engine.js` · Field vision as a yard-a-point radius from 75 (`visionRadiusV96`, `TU("visionRadiusFrom")`).
- `v87 WHO IS ON HIM` / `v87 THE QB SEES THE LANE` — `src/04-engine.js` · Coverage by alignment (no coin-flip credit), opportunity scrambles, no backward throws. `v87check`.
- `v82 THE FRONT HAS A PLAN` / `v82 DISGUISE` / `v82 SPECIAL TEAMS` / `v82 BALL SKILLS` — `src/04-engine.js` · Stunts, spy, protection calls, chips, rotation/press, the pocket, ball skills, bounce/pile, effort, the back's lookahead, leverage, and punts/kickoffs/FGs as agent plays. `readcheck` (pure Node), `movementcheck`.
- `v81 BALL AWARENESS` / `v81 THE POINT OF ATTACK` — `src/04-engine.js` · Defenders find the ball on their own clock, fakes, committed pursuit lines, rolled blocks, the hole. `readcheck`.
- `v139 THREE MOVES, NOT TWO` / `v139 THE LUNGE HAS A SIZE` — `src/04-engine.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · A committed dive is sized off its own closing force (`window.__V139.lunge`); the carrier beats a man by spin / juke / side step off the roll the whiff already took. `movecheck`.
- `GRIDIRON FieldSim — agent-based play resolution` — `src/04-engine.js` · THE play resolver: agents, contact/tackling, and stat truth (`who ACTUALLY made the stop`). Local coords: LOS at `lx = 0`, offense attacks +x, `YD = 5.88` px/yd, `TICK = 33` ms. Breakdown in ARCHITECTURE.md.
- `GRIDIRON play choreography engine` — `src/04-engine.js` · The legacy render-only choreographer (`buildPlayScript`), the fallback when no FieldSim log matches; cosmetic, never stats.
- `RIB_TUNE: every gameplay dial` — `src/04-engine.js` · `TU(key, default)` — every gameplay number reads through it; retune live with `window.RIB_TUNE[key] = …`.

### 1b. The game engine, the booking, the season sim (`src/07-career-app.js`)

- `v166 J THE FILM ROOM` — `src/07-career-app.js` · `repV166J` above `dcPlanV165D`, and its `keyYou`. His reputation is the mean `perf` of his last four played weeks (`player.weekResults`, read at kickoff, never written) from `repFloorV166J` over `repSpanV166J`; the defense facing him starts its key there (× `repKeyKV166J` × `dcIqV165D`). Kill switch `v166Jfilm`. `window.__V166J` (`rep`); `repcheck`.
- `v166 I FOURTH DOWN BY THE NUMBERS` — `src/07-career-app.js` · The `down === 4` branch of the kick decisions. A staff past `gmIqV166I` (`dcIqV165D`) prices go / punt / kick in expected points (`epBaseV166I` + `epPerYdV166I` a yard; conversion odds from distance and the line-vs-front edge; the punt's net; the make odds by distance, less the kickoff's worth), with a risk premium on going (`gmRiskV166I`) and the late-game lean (`gmLateGoV166I`, `gmLateSafeV166I`); younger staffs keep the old rule. Kill switch `v166Igm`. `window.__V166I` (`decisions`, `go`, opt-in `log`); `gmcheck`.
- `v166 H THE OFFENSE HAS A COORDINATOR` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Beside `window.__V165D` (`ocMemV166H`, `ocReadV166H`), the record after v165 D's call, the concept chain, `paV81` and v165 E's play-mix lean. Each offense remembers the calls it has faced (blitzes, zone, man, the run key) and, past `ocWarmV166H` snaps, its coordinator (`dcIqV165D`) answers: screens and quick game against the blitz (`ocBlitzKV166H`), shots against man (`ocManShotKV166H`), play action and passing into a run key (`ocPaKeyV166H`, `ocPassKeyV166H`), draws against a pass key (`ocDrawKV166H`). Odds only, no draws. Kill switch `v166Hoc`. `window.__V166H`, opt-in `window.__V166Hlog`; `occheck`.
- `v165 G THE BOOTH SEES IT` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Right after v165 D's record, before `mkPlay`. When the coordinator's call decided the play the row's text says so — the blitz that got home (a sack on a called blitz), a run stuffed at or behind the line into a run key ≥ `boothRunKeyV165G`, a 15-yard play fake against a run key ≥ `boothPaKeyV165G`, the hot read off the blitz (v165 E), and "They're keying on YOU" (keyYou ≥ `boothKeyYouV165G`, once a quarter, `dcBoothQV165G`). Never on a score, a turnover or a pick six; the words avoid the SACK / INTERCEPT / First down the box score and renderer test for. Kill switch `v165Gbooth`. `window.__V165D.notes`; `dccheck`.
- `v165 E THE AUDIBLE` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Right after v165 D's call, before `cTag`. A QB whose accessor awareness clears `audPivotV165E` sees the look with a chance that climbs over `audSpanV165E` to `audMaxV165E` (one Math.random a snap, only when a coordinator called it and the QB can see anything) and checks a dropback or a shot: into a called blitz → the hot read (a quick call off `pickPlayV101`; v165 D's blitz pressure skips the quick game); into a run key ≥ `audRunKeyV165E` on an early down → play action. The row opens "Audible —". The staff's play mix also goes with what is working: `passP` leans by the game's YPA against YPC × the coordinator sense (`ocLeanKV165E`, after `ocWarmPlaysV165E` snaps; kill switch `v165Eoc`). `playV101` and `paV81` are `let` now. Kill switch `v165Eaud`. `window.__V165D.audibles`, the opt-in log's `audible` rows; `dccheck`.
- `v165 D THE DEFENSE HAS A COORDINATOR` — `src/07-career-app.js` + `src/04-engine.js` · 07: above `simGameV2`'s run resolver `B` (`dcMemV165D`, `dcPlanV165D`, `dcRecordV165D`), the call right after `paV81`, the record right before `mkPlay`. Each defense remembers THIS game by the side that defends and calls every snap blind: `runKey` (−1 pass … +1 run, from run share and YPC against YPA, × `dcIqLvlV165D` by level × how much it has seen, `dcWarmPlaysV165D`), `keyYou` (his share of the yards past `dcYouShareV165D`), and a called `blitz` (by down and distance, less against a sharp QB, `dcQbRespectKV165D`; one Math.random a snap). The blitz adds `dcBlitzPressV165D` to the pass pressure (not on a quick or a screen). Both FieldSim calls pass `opts.dcV165D`. 04: the blitzer is the call (the old 18% draw still spent); `dcReadKV165D` moves the LB/S read clock on runs (`dcRunKeyReadV165D`, `dcPassKeyReadV165D`, `dcRunBlitzReadV165D`, `dcKeyYouReadV165D`); a run key bites on the fake (`dcBiteV165D`), drops late (`dcDropLateMsV165D`) and opens play action (`dcPaSepV165D`); a pass key sits on routes (`dcPassKeySepV165D`); a blitz is a hot read for a QB who sees it and a late read for one who does not (`dcHotSepV165D` × his IQ from 55; the chosen window by call is counted in `root.__V165B.dcSep*`); `keyYou` brackets him (`dcBracketYouV165D`). Kill switch `v165Ddc`. `window.__V165D` (`mem`, `plan`, `iq`, `last`, `calls`, `blitzes`), opt-in `window.__V165Dlog`; `dccheck`.
- `v165 A THE MIND HAS NO CEILING` — `src/07-career-app.js` · At `simGameV2`'s accessor `_` (the helpers `MIND_KEYS_V165A` / `mindHeadV165A` sit above `youSimAttrs`). For the you-player the mind keys (awareness, vision, discipline) skip the carrier ceiling (`simKneeTopPosV141`) and the star dampener — a back's vision used to stop at 72, under the 75 where the read radius starts. Asked with `head` (FieldSim's third argument), they ease to `99 + iqHeadV165A` over `iqHeadTailV165A` instead of 99 (sheet 400 → ~116, 999 → ~131 at the UFF). Body keys, AI men and every 07 roll are unchanged. Kill switch `v165Amind`. `window.__V165A`; `iqcheck`.
- `v153 A THE STAT GAIN LANDS` — `src/07-career-app.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · Beside `flMinorV96`, called from `liveTick`'s play-end callback. `statGainPlayV153A` diffs the booked running line (`t.stat`) against the last shown one (`liveCtl._gainV153A`, from `statGainStartV153A`): the gains (`GAIN_V153A`; a catch and its yards are one), the milestones (`milestonesV153A`: `mileRushV153A` ...) and a new single-game career high (`gameHighsV153A` on the player, seeded from this season's lines; `careerHighMinV153A`). `statGainShowV153A` flies each callout from `__ribYouClientV153A()` (05: his marker in client px) into its tile, which counts up (`countUpV153A`, the tile's target in `data-v153t` so the next play never fights it), with `statGainSfxV153A` (through `sfxOutV151E` → `RIB_MUSIC.sfxOut`) and `ribHaptics.impact` LIGHT / MEDIUM. Presentation only. `TU("statGainV153A", 0)` is the old flash. `window.__V153A_LIVE`, `__V153A_LIVE_API`; `v153Acheck`.

- `v141 EVERY STAT IS ON THE FIELD` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Beside `youSimAttrs()` (`qr`), before `buildGameRosters` (`Wr`). All eighteen sheet keys reach the FieldSim agent: `youSimAttrs()` and `buildGameRosters`' inner `h()` emit every key `makeAgents` asks for (nine used to fall to a flat 45); `simScaleV141` puts a sheet value on OVR's scale past the wall (250 → ~116, 350 → ~172); the accessor's fallback is the man's own OVR; `kneeV141` eases the normalise to 99; `TU("starFloorMinV141", .6)` floors `_starScale`; durability is `dur` on the agent (`durKeepV141`). The you-player at RB / WR / TE eases toward a lower ceiling (`simKneeTopPosV141`, 72/76/76). Dials `reactBaseMs`, `aiQbThrowBumpV141`, `composurePivotV141`, `simKneeV141`, `simKneeTailV141`, `simKneeTopV141`, `durKeepK`, `injResistPastK`, `injResistFloor`; kill switches `v141Keys` / `v141Fallback`. **`h()` returns via a comma chain — keep `return b` live if you touch its tail.** `window.__V141`; `v141check`, `equaltalentcheck`.
- `v109 THE GAME HAS A CLOCK` — `src/07-career-app.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` · The extra point is its own row (`pushTryV109`, same rolls); `clockStopped`/`secs`/`runoff`/`toLeft`/`period` ride every row; out-of-bounds is read from the sim log first; two-minute warning / period / coin toss / timeout are rows; the flag is thrown, announced and re-spotted; a close spot brings the chains. `v109Dcheck`.
- `v103 KEEP THE PICTURE` — `src/07-career-app.js` · `retagSimLog(y)` re-tags a queued log when `dampV76` reshapes the yards. **Any post-sim yardage reshape must call it, or the play loses its animation.**
- `v101 THE PLAYBOOK` — `src/07-career-app.js` · `PLAYBOOK_V101` / `pickPlayV101`: 42 named calls feeding `opts.gap` (runs) and `opts.routes` (passes) into FieldSim. `v101check`, `routecheck`.
- `v87 SAFETY` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Tackled in his own end zone.
- `v85 THE WHEEL SPINS IN THE BACKGROUND` — `src/07-career-app.js` · The silent week (quick play, sim the rest) and live-game booking: `silentWeekV85`. `v85check`.
- `v76 MARGIN CURVE` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Five banners: where a scoreline comes from (ARCHITECTURE.md, "The margin curve"). `blowoutcheck`.
- `v16 EMERGENT GAME ENGINE` — `src/07-career-app.js` · `simGameV2` (`Yr`, `window.__simGameV2`): full games, play-calling, the you-player's box score `P`. `simcheck`, `creditcheck`, `statcreditcheck`.

## 2. Renderer — the broadcast, camera, field and stadium (`src/05-field-renderer.js`)

<!-- new renderer entries go here, newest first -->
- `v194 D IDLE V2` / `v194 D THE HUDDLE FACES IN` / `v194 D THE STIFF ARM` / `v194 D THE TURF REMEMBERS ITS PAINT` — `src/05-field-renderer.js` · IDLE V2 (after `idleBetweenV144`): `idlePlanV194D(P)` (called from `startPostV86`) gives every free man at the whistle his own start (`idleStaggerMsV194D`, linemen `idleLineLagMsV194D` later), pace, gait (jog / walk / linemen's trudge / DB backpedal / the winners' swagger / the losers' droop — who won is `idleOutcomeV194D`), a gesture (fist pump, point, shake out, a pat on a teammate) and an end pose (hands on hips / on knees / head down / watching the spot); `idleGatherV194D` runs it inside the post-whistle gather, `idleOfferV194D` holds a v109 helper's hand out until the man is up, `idleBetweenV194D` replaces v144 C's shuffle between the plays (the pose held; steps, shakes, looks on each man's own clock — **every step still off `_idleHomeV144`, the whistle's spot**). The poses are v193 AJ clips (`idle194_*`), every choice from `rngV194D` (seeded, never `Math.random`), cleared at the snap (`idleResetV194D`); `v194Didle`. THE HUDDLE FACES IN (`hudFaceInV194D`, in `update`'s v87 huddle branch): every man in the hold — the QB too — faces the middle of his huddle-mates' spots on every frame (linemen held "idle"); `v194Dhuddle`. THE STIFF ARM (`stiffArmV194D`, from the `stiffarm` case and from a `brokenTackle` with `stiffArm`): only sim-graded events; the arm out on the man's screen side (the stiff cells only point sideways), locked (`stiffLockMsV194D`), the man drawn ON the palm (`stiffReachCellV194D`, the anchor's keyframes re-read off the carrier every locked frame) with his head snapped back, then he staggers (`stiffStaggerMsV194D`) or goes down (`drop`) and gets up; the flash and starburst on his facemask (`stiffFlashV194D`), a shake, a freeze — a `sick` one freezes `stiffSickFreezeMsV194D`, punches the zoom, slows the clock and calls "SICK STIFF ARM!"; `v194Dstiff`. THE TURF REMEMBERS ITS PAINT: `paintSnapV194D` (in `warpField`, before the dirt and v98's wash) keeps a copy of the turf as painted; `turfPaintAtV194D(x, y)` reads a patch at the spot's projected pixel, votes paint / grass / dirt (`paintClassV194D`) and shades it (`paintShadesV194D`); `addDirtV193AG` stores it on the mark (`d.paint`) — the scuff's lip (and, on paint, its core) and `dirtBurstV193AG`'s clods and dust take that colour; `v194Dpaint`. Kill switch `TU("v194D", 0)` for all four. `window.__V194D` (`idle`, `huddle`, `stiff`, `paint`, `paintAt(x, y)`); `v194Dcheck`.
- `v194 C THE LIGHTS STAY UP` / `v194 C THE END-ZONE CAMERA` / `v194 C THE JUMBO WEARS THE JERSEY` — `src/05-field-renderer.js` + `src/28-cosmetics.js` · **LIGHTS** (after `v194C()`, beside `camKickSpotV194C`): `farStandsV193AG` leaves the skyline it built in `crowd.skyV194C` (the canopy's top edge `at(p, FR)` along the whole chain, its depth, its build stamp); in `buildStadiumV92`'s v164 A branch `mastsOnRoofV194C(far)` stands each far mast ON it — the pole's foot on the highest roof point under the pole (`MAST_POLE_V194C`, the sheet's pole columns per face) sunk `mastRoofSinkV194C` 3 px, but never so low that the lamp bank (`MAST_HEAD_LIFT_V194C` of the height above the foot) dips under the skyline anywhere under the mast (`mastRoofSpanV194C` 0.5 of its width each side + `mastRoofPadV194C` 6 px); x and size stay v164 A's field spot. `lightBehindV194C()` puts a far rig's glow and beam `lightBehindV194C` 0.02 behind the far stands (pools unchanged). Off the far stands (v193 AG 0) or with the switch off: v164 A's feet in the lower bowl. Kill `TU("v194Clights", 0)`; `window.__V194C.lights` (`on`, `masts`, `skyline()`). **CAMERA** (in `camFocusV112`'s pre-snap kick branch): `camKickSpotV194C(P, k, glide)` frames a fg / xp / punt / kickoff during the glide-in on the kicker's first scripted frame through this snap's `PJ`, not on the man still jogging from the last play's spot (which the re-anchored perspective puts deep in the foreground band, s ≈ 2-3 — the dive near the end zone). Kill `TU("v194Ccam", 0)`; `window.__V194C.cam` (`kickSpots`, `lastKick`). **JUMBO** (28, after `kitV161A`): `kitV161A` carries `U` (`decoUV194C(kitKey)`: the resolved uniform the field painted, its `kitDeco(U, null)` cached by stamp) when `dressedV194C(kitKey)` ("you" when he wears something, "off" when v159 A dressed the team — `__V159A_FIELD.kit`; never "def"); `frameCanvasV161A` / `frameCanvasV177I` paint it on each recoloured frame (`decoFrameV194C`, before the skin); `boardCel.tex` keeps the helmet for a dressed teammate; `noteKitV194C`. Kill `TU("v194Cjumbo", 0)`; `window.__V194C.jumbo` (`kits`, `last`). `v194Ccheck` (v92check / v98check read the roof placement).

- `v164 A THE LIGHTS STAND WHERE THEY STAND` / `v164 A THE SHADOW IS THE MAN` — `src/05-field-renderer.js` · Before `keyLightV99`. THE MASTS: `mastsV164()` stands EIGHT masts at fixed FIELD positions (`mastBackPxV164` behind each end line, lateral `mastWideV164` / `mastInnerV164` of the half-width) and projects them through `crowdProject` on every bake (`buildStadiumV92`, `ST.mastsV164`): the far four are drawn at the bowl's corners, scaled by the bowl's own perspective (`mastKRefV164`, clamped `mastScaleMinV164`..`mastScaleMaxV164`), feet tucked above the bowl's foot (`mastFootDropV164`, never on the grass); the near four are a light only. Each mast's beam aims at and its pool lies on its own patch of the field (`mastPoolInYdV164`, `mastAimInYdV164`, `mastPoolLatV164`; `tw._aimX/_aimY/_poolX/_poolY`), and `lightFieldV98` bakes the pools at the same projected points (`nearPoolAV164`). The key light is a PHYSICAL mast (`keyMastV164` = 2, the east end's inner mast on the bottom touchline): drawn and far on an offensive drive, behind the camera on a defensive one (`keyLightV99` → `{phys, far, i: -1}`), where `shadowVecV99` throws every shadow UP the screen, shorter (`shadowUpKV164`) and raking hardest at the far end. Before this the masts were planted at screen fractions and the key was "tower 2", so the lights and the shadows swapped sides on every change of possession and slid against the bowl as the LOS moved. THE SHADOW: `castSilV164(m, gx, gy, lift, spdPx, down, o)` replaces the ellipse with HIS OWN SILHOUETTE — `silTexV164(key)` blacks out the body's frame into a cached `sil164_<key>` canvas texture; `m.sil` (origin at the feet) is flipped over them (`silLenKV164` × the rake), rotated along the shadow vector, `silAV164`; `m.sil2` is the second lamp's fainter one (`fillVecV101`, `silFillAV164`); the old ellipse is the contact blob (`silBlobA`, wider over a man on the ground, who throws no silhouette); `silBindV164` rebinds the frame at the END of `placeMarker` / `placeRef` (the run cycle changes it after the cast). Officials cast the same way. `__V99.man(i)` reports the silhouette in the ellipse's terms so `v99check` still reads it (updated: the key holds still WITHIN a bake, a behind-the-camera key is `i = -1`). Kill switches `v164Alights` / `v164Asil` (and `silFillV164`). `window.__V164A` (`masts`, `key`, `sil`, `man`, `vdir`); `v164Acheck`.

- `v163 A THE GAME NEVER STOPS (renderer)` / `v163 A THE GAME NEVER STOPS (the watch on every play)` / `v163 A THE GAME NEVER STOPS (the live loop)` — `src/05-field-renderer.js` + `src/06-phaser-launcher.js` + `src/07-career-app.js` · Before the field bridge `class Dt` (05), before the `GridironPhaser` object (06) and before `liveTick` (07). Phaser 3.90's loop re-requests a frame only AFTER the frame's callback returns, so one thrown frame ended the loop, the play and (through its completion) the live game. 05: `guardLoopV163A(game)` wraps `game.loop.callback` and every callback `loop.start` is handed (wake keeps it); a thrown frame goes to `frameErrorV163A` (unwinds the canvas — `restore()` ×32, identity transform, `currentContext` back to the game's — and `sweepV163A` re-dresses or hides anything whose texture source is gone). `rebindOnSwapV163A(game)`: on the texture manager's `addtexture`, sprites still holding a destroyed texture of that key are rebound in a microtask (a texture re-registered under the same key — kits, crowd canvases, number fonts, celebrations — used to crash the canvas renderer with `reading 'drawImage'`). 06: `watchPlayV163A(fin)` — a wall-clock interval per play, counting only while the page is visible: the loop's frame count still for `v163AstallMs` (4 s) → `loop.wake()` restart, again → release; no scene holding the play's completion for `v163AorphanMs` (5 s) → release; booting past `v163AbootMs` (12 s) → release; `v163AplayMaxMs` (120 s) → release. Release = `softStop` the scene's play and `fin()`; `fin` is idempotent; `cancel` clears the watch. 07: `liveTick` is a guard around `liveTickBodyV163A`; each continuation sets `liveCtl.contV163A`, and a throw before one is scheduled retries in 250 ms (max 20 in a row); the play's completion catches its scoreboard work and always schedules the tick; the header branch schedules its tick BEFORE `yl`. Kill switch `TU("v163A", 0)` (the loop guard is read at mount). `window.__V163A` (`caught`, `swept`, `rebound`, `restarts`, `released`, `releases`, `tickErrors`, `errors`); `v163Acheck`.
- `v162 A THE HANDOVER IS SMOOTH` — `src/05-field-renderer.js` · Beside `drawField` (`fieldKeyV162A`), plus `renderStatic`, `staticFormation`, `refreshPersp`, `crowdSection` and `updateCrowd`. `drawField` keeps the key of the bake on screen (the spot, `VDIR`, `__FIELD_FX`, the weather / day, the light dial, `RIB_TUNE`, the opponent's palette, the stadium tier / week, the art) in `_fieldKeyV162A`; the same key redraws only the LOS / first-down lines — `warpField`, `buildCrowd` and `buildSideline` are skipped. Only a COMPLETE bake is kept (art still decoding → the next call builds). `refreshPersp` clears the key (an explicit re-bake: a slider, the v44 crest, late art). `renderStatic` clears `_wxV144` and the `#field` CSS transform itself instead of calling `applyFieldFx` (which re-baked the same field). `staticFormation` dresses the header's men by `kitForV105_2` — dressed by side, his team's defense wore the opponent's kit on a defensive drive header and `highlight` recoloured his textures into the opponent's palette and back at the snap. - `v153 B MY PLAYS ARE MY SIDE OF THE BALL` — `src/07-career-app.js` + `public/rib-menu-coach.js` · My Plays Only is free (`playsOnlyOkV151A` answers yes) and ON by default: `freshState` ships `onlyInvolved: true`, `playsDefaultV153B` (called from `settingOn`) switches an old save on once (`settings.playsDefaultV153B`); `vl` → `playsSkipV153B(row)` shows the you-player's side of the ball (offense for QB/RB/WR/TE/OL, defense otherwise), special teams only when he is in the play, any involved play always. The coach's `live` stop says it. Kill switch `v153Bplays` (the v151 A gate and the strictly-involved filter). `v153Bcheck`, `v151Acheck`.
- `v151 A THE FREE GAME IS THE GAME, AND THE STORE IS A LADDER` — `src/27-monetize.js` · The owner's hybrid model, still behind `MONETIZE_ENABLED = false`: three nested tiers (`rib.noads` $3.99 ⊂ `rib.pro` $8.99 ⊂ `rib.founder` $14.99, one key each, the rest resolved by `IMPLIES`; `rib.upgrade.*` at the difference), rewarded CONVENIENCES only (`speed4` 20 min / `simUnlimited` 30 min of uncounted season sims (v156 B; was `simExtra` +1 skip) / `cosTrial` 24 h; 4 a day, TU `adsPerDayV151A` ≤ 5; Ad Free claims them without the ad), the pass `rib.pass.<seasonId>` → `pass:<seasonId>` + `RIB_SEASONS.grantPremium` (`purchasePass`), packs `rib.cos.<packId>` → `cos:<item>` + `RIB_COSMETICS.grant`, `unlock_all_team_style` → `cos:team_style_all`, expansions `exp:<id>` listed COMING SOON and never sold, and **the guard** (`validateProduct` / `keyAllowed`: any grant outside the allow-list — PP, prestige, stats, rolls, gear, wheels — is refused at load, at purchase and in `grant()`). The PP double is retired (`claimPayoutBoost` → 0). The store is one ladder (`.mz151-*`, one inner scroller, 400x860). `docs/MONETIZATION.md`; `v151Acheck`.

- `v152 A.2 THE BAND PLAYS ON ARRIVAL` — `src/30-music.js` · The owner wants the anthem on load, not on the first tap. `autoStart()` makes the context at load and ASKS: running (a trusted site, an installed app) → `M.gestured = true`, `play('auto')`, `state().auto = 'web'`; in the Capacitor shell it just plays (`'native'` — Capacitor already disables the gesture rule in both web views: `Bridge.java` `setMediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture(false)`, `CAPBridgeViewController` `mediaTypesRequiringUserActionForPlayback = []`); refused → `'refused'`, nothing sounds, the context waits suspended and the first tap starts it as before. A synthetic click cannot unlock audio (not a user activation), so none is faked. `v151Echeck` (both paths).
- `v151 E THE BAND PLAYS` — `src/30-music.js` + `src/07-career-app.js` + `src/05-field-renderer.js` + `public/rib-menu-coach.js` + `public/rib-vault-audio.js` + `index.html` · The music and every sound level. `window.RIB_MUSIC` (30, loaded after `29-seasons.js` and before the baked menu block): the owner's anthem (`public/audio/brass_anthem.m4a` — Opus in MP4, 48 kHz stereo, 153.57 s; `brass_anthem.mp3` a 128 kbps fallback, never precached) STREAMED, never decoded: the compressed file is fetched once as a Blob (through the service worker, so it plays offline) and two `<audio>` decks play it, each through a `MediaElementAudioSourceNode` and its own gain into fade → duck → master. The file opens on ~160 ms of silence and ends on a ringing note, so the loop points are measured constants (`LOOP_START` 0.161, `LOOP_END` 153.313): `swapStart` starts the parked deck FROM 0 (its silent lead-in absorbs the element's start latency) when the playing deck is `LOOP_START` short of `LOOP_END`, and the crossfade (outgoing cosine over `XFADE_S` 250 ms, incoming sine over `XFADE_IN_S` 40 ms) is fired when the new deck's playhead actually crosses `LOOP_START` (`state().swaps`). Fallbacks: `mode: "buffer"` (decode on a 24 kHz context, splice in place) when an element cannot be routed, `mode: "element"` with no WebAudio. Loudness `NORM` .812 (−18.2 → −20 dBFS). Starts on the first trusted gesture (both decks played inside it — iOS), fetched only once the page is idle, fades in, pauses both decks and suspends on a hidden tab / Capacitor `pause`. **Other sound paths opt in explicitly**: `RIB_MUSIC.sfxOut(ctx)` (07's `sfxOutV151E` for `playSfx` and the growth blips, 05's stingers, the coach's voice master, the vault master) returns one gain + analyser per context — the effects volume, MUTE ALL and the DUCK (.42 while anything else sounds); before 30 loads, or on any throw, the answer is `ctx.destination` (the old path). Prefs in localStorage `rib.music.v151`, not the save; `settings.sound` and `rib.coachVoice.v119` keep their keys. Settings › SOUND is `soundCardV151E()` in 07 (drawn from storage — v140), tabbed by `22`'s `snd-v151e` class; handlers `ribSoundV151E` / `ribMuteToggleV151E` (hoisted, in 30), `coachVoiceV151E` (07); the quick mute is `#muteV151E` in the career top bar and `.mute-v151e.menu-v151e` mounted on the menu's `.rib9-topbar`. `window.RIB_MUSIC` (`state()`, `loopInfo()`, `sfxOut()`, `_seek()`, `_rms()`); `v151Echeck`.
- `v150 C THE HOOKS ARE IN, THE SWITCH IS STILL OFF` — `src/07-career-app.js` · Beside `bankPPV136` (plus `public/rib-menu.js` `storeTileV150C` and `src/26-platform.js` `back()`). The monetization hooks H1–H11 of `docs/MONETIZATION.md` §8, each behind `mzV150C()` (null unless `RIB_MONETIZE.enabled`), so OFF is the identity: `setSpeed` refuses a locked speed into `RIB_MONETIZE.speedLocked` (H1), `speed3V150C` adds a 3× button only with `features.speed3` (H2), `speedClampV150C` in `startLivePlayback` (H3), `payoutBoostV150C` in the `screenGameOver` / `screenWin` settles (H4/H5; `bankPPV136` deliberately unboosted, H6), `cosOkV150C` gates NEW `cos:`-tagged camera/weather entries only (H8), the STORE menu tile (H9), `storeRowV150C` in Settings › SAVE (H10), back closes the store's sheets first (H11); save slots (H7) documented only. **New hook code must stay the identity while the switch is off.** `window.__V150C`; `v150Ccheck.mjs`.
- `v149 E THE STORE IS WIRED, AND SWITCHED OFF` — `src/27-monetize.js` · `window.RIB_MONETIZE`. **`MONETIZE_ENABLED` ships `false`, and while false the file must stay a complete no-op** (API object only: no wrapper, DOM, style, timer, listener, observer or storage write — `v149Echeck` diffs it against a boot with the file blocked). ON (`?monetize=1` / `rib.monetize.dev.v149` on dev hosts only, or `window.RIB_MONETIZE_CONFIG`) it wraps `window.setSpeed` (4× needs `speed4`) and `window.buy` (analytics only) and adds the speed-row offer, the career-end "double this payout" chip, the topbar STORE chip and the store screen (`.mz149-*`). Entitlements live in `rib.monetize.ents.v1`, **never in the save**. Never sell gear, rolls, wheel spins, PP or a setting that is free today. `docs/MONETIZATION.md` (§8: the hooks still to add).
- `v149 D IT INSTALLS` — `src/26-platform.js` · Loaded LAST; `pwa/sw.js`; `scripts/lib/pwa.mjs`. Builds add `<meta name="rib-sw">` + `sw.js` (content-hashed precache, network-first navigation); the worker registers only on a built site. `ribDialog` (use it for new confirms/prompts — old call sites in `docs/APP-STORE.md` §13), `ribSave` (file export/import, rolling backups `rib_backup_v149_*`), `ribHaptics`, Android back (`__PLATFORM_V149.back.parent/.hold`), keep-awake. **It wraps `GridironStorage.save` — keep saves going through it.** Icons `scripts/build-app-icons.py`. `window.__PLATFORM_V149`; `v149Dcheck`.

## 8. Loading — the splash, the film, the live game's loader (`src/03-splash.js`, `index.html`)

<!-- new loading entries go here, newest first -->

- `v132 THE INTRO FILM IS THE LOADER, AND IT IS UP ON THE FIRST PAINT` / `v132 THE FILM IS ALWAYS WARM` — `src/03-splash.js` · The live game's loader has NO chase: `.rib-liveload-v94.film` from the first paint, the still `rib_film_v116.jpg` (`.rib-liveload-still-v132`) under everything, the film over it from the seam (`.playing`); `__V114.warm()` builds a standby element and `take()` hands out parked-then-standby; the door opens on `item.ready` after `LIVE_MIN_MS` / `LIVE_SEEN_MS` / `LIVE_FILM_WAIT_MS`. `__LIVELOAD_V94.lastStillMs` / `.lastPicMs`, `__V114.standby`; `v115check`, `v127check`.
- `v127:` — `src/03-splash.js` · Door two can load its own film (no banner; grep `v127:`): with nothing parked it builds its own `<video>` on `__V114`'s source, seeks to the seam on `loadedmetadata` and claims the stage only at `atSeam()`; its audition is longer; nothing is built when `F.off` / `F.rm`. `__LIVELOAD_V94.lastFilmOwn`; `v127check`.
- `v121 THE FOOTBALL IS THE LAST RESORT` — `src/03-splash.js` · Beside `mountChase()`. The 🏈 is `display:none` until `ballStandsIn()` adds `#splash.ball` (film stood down AND the v91 sheet failed). `splashcheck`.
- `v116 THE FILM LOOPS` — `src/03-splash.js` · `LOOP_FROM_V116` (6.5s) is the seam; `loop` stays FALSE on the element (a native loop rewinds to black and swallows `ended`) — `loopBack(v)` does it by hand, shared as `__V114.loopBack`. The door waits for `V.landed` (`FILM_WAIT_INTRO`). Asset `public/rib_film_v116.{mp4,webm,jpg}` cut by `scripts/build-splash-film.mjs` (`+faststart`, or it is not a loading screen). `v114check`.
- `v115 THE FILM AT BOTH DOORS` — `src/03-splash.js` · The live loader plays the sting as a backdrop (`.rib-liveload-film-v115`, `object-fit:contain`), starting at `LIVE_FILM_FROM`, on the element door one loaded (`__V114.park()` / `take()` / `give()` — one decoded film a session); `claimed()` fires BEFORE the seek. No film at door one means none here either. `v115check`.
- `v114 THE SPLASH IS A FILM` — `src/03-splash.js` + `index.html` · The source is chosen by a picker inline beside the `<video>` (a `<source>` or preload would start a fetch the page cannot take back); `stopLoad()`; the splash leaves when the app is ready AND the intro has landed (`FILM_CAP_MS`, `FILM_START_MS` audition, `?noFilmV114`). The bar: v112 A's compositor sweep over a determinate fill. `window.__V114`; `v114check`.
- `v112 A THE CHASE IS ALWAYS READY` — `index.html` · The v91 loading sheet is requested in the first breath of the head, before the CSS; v94's `load()` adopts those promises (exactly two requests a session); the bar sweeps on the compositor. `window.__V112_A`, `?noWarmV112`; `v112Acheck`.
- `v97: the loader goes first` — `src/06-phaser-launcher.js` · The live sim waits at the loader's door (`__LIVELOAD_V94.whenClear`).
- `v94 THE CHASE` — `src/03-splash.js` · One canvas chase engine from the v91 sheet (`window.__CHASE_V94.make()`); `window.__splashDoneV94()` is the door `goView` uses; `window.__LIVELOAD_V94` mounts the loader over `.field-wrap`. `splashcheck`.
